### OWNED-AUDIT 16: run_prompt_review_v1.py

Original LF rows:
1: """Run one prompt-only configured subscription review; preserve all outcomes and stop with SIGTERM only."""
2: import argparse
3: import hashlib
4: import json
5: import os
6: from pathlib import Path
7: import signal
8: import subprocess
9: import time
10: 
11: RUN = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
12: REGISTRY = Path('/Users/jinleic/jinleic-workspace/docs/knowledge-system/DELEGATE_MODELS.json')
13: 
14: 
15: def clock():
16:     return subprocess.check_output(['/bin/date', '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'], text=True).strip()
17: 
18: 
19: def write_new(path, value):
20:     with path.open('x') as handle:
21:         json.dump(value, handle, indent=2)
22:         handle.write('\n')
23:         handle.flush()
24:         os.fsync(handle.fileno())
25: 
26: 
27: def main():
28:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
29:     parser.add_argument('prompt')
30:     parser.add_argument('--budget-seconds', type=int, default=2400)
31:     args = parser.parse_args()
32:     prompt_path = Path(args.prompt).resolve()
33:     if not prompt_path.is_relative_to(RUN) or not 60 <= args.budget_seconds <= 3600:
34:         raise SystemExit('Invalid scoped prompt or self-budget')
35:     base = prompt_path.with_suffix('')
36:     paths = {key: Path(str(base) + suffix) for key, suffix in
37:              [('stdout', '.stdout.json'), ('stderr', '.stderr.txt'), ('receipt', '.receipt.json'),
38:               ('note', '.note.md'), ('launch', '.launch.json')]}
39:     if any(p.exists() for p in paths.values()):
40:         raise SystemExit('Refusing to overwrite review evidence')
41:     cfg = json.loads(REGISTRY.read_text())['claude']
42:     cmd = ['claude', '--model', cfg['model'], '--effort', cfg['effort'], '--safe-mode', '--no-chrome',
43:            '--tools', '', '--permission-mode', 'dontAsk', '--no-session-persistence',
44:            '--output-format', 'json', '-p', prompt_path.read_text()]
45:     env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1', TMPDIR=str(RUN / 'control/tmp'),
46:                TMP=str(RUN / 'control/tmp'), TEMP=str(RUN / 'control/tmp'))
47:     begin = time.monotonic()
48:     record = {'started_at': clock(), 'requested_model': cfg['model'], 'requested_effort': cfg['effort'],
49:               'prompt_sha256': hashlib.sha256(prompt_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
50:               'source_sha256': hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(), 'tools': [],
51:               'self_budget_seconds': args.budget_seconds, 'status': 'NO_VERDICT'}
52:     with paths['stdout'].open('x') as stdout, paths['stderr'].open('x') as stderr:
53:         proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=RUN, env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
54:                                 stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, start_new_session=True)
55:         write_new(paths['launch'], dict(record, pid=proc.pid, pgid=os.getpgid(proc.pid), cwd=str(RUN),
56:                                        argv_sha256=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(cmd).encode()).hexdigest()))
57:         while proc.poll() is None and time.monotonic() - begin < args.budget_seconds:
58:             time.sleep(1)
59:         if proc.poll() is None:
60:             record['stop_requested_at'] = clock()
61:             record['status'] = 'SELF_BUDGET_SIGTERM_NO_VERDICT'
62:             os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
63:             settle_end = time.monotonic() + 60
64:             while proc.poll() is None and time.monotonic() < settle_end:
65:                 time.sleep(0.2)
66:             if proc.poll() is None:
67:                 record['unsettled_pid'] = proc.pid
68:         record['returncode'] = proc.poll()
69:     if record['returncode'] == 0 and 'stop_requested_at' not in record:
70:         try:
71:             events = json.loads(paths['stdout'].read_text())
72:             if isinstance(events, dict):
73:                 events = [events]
74:             finals = [e for e in events if e.get('type') == 'result']
75:             final = finals[-1]
76:             record['reported_init_models'] = sorted({e['model'] for e in events if e.get('type') == 'system' and e.get('model')})
77:             record['reported_model_usage'] = final.get('modelUsage', {})
78:             if final.get('subtype') == 'success' and not final.get('is_error', False):
79:                 chunks = [b['text'] for e in events if e.get('type') == 'assistant'
80:                           for b in e.get('message', {}).get('content', [])
81:                           if b.get('type') == 'text' and b.get('text')]
82:                 note = '\n\n'.join(chunks) if chunks else final.get('result', '')
83:                 with paths['note'].open('x') as handle:
84:                     handle.write(note.rstrip() + '\n')
85:                 record['status'] = 'REVIEW_RETURNED_UNVERIFIED'
86:         except (ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, OSError) as exc:
87:             record['parse_error'] = str(exc)
88:     record['finished_at'] = clock()
89:     record['duration_seconds'] = time.monotonic() - begin
90:     write_new(paths['receipt'], record)
91:     print(json.dumps(record, indent=2), flush=True)
92:     return 0 if record['status'] == 'REVIEW_RETURNED_UNVERIFIED' else 1
93: 
94: 
95: if __name__ == '__main__':
96:     raise SystemExit(main())
